  "figures": [
    {
      "caption": "The two paths are followed outward from a fixed central position. Matched indices need not be equally far from the center; their derivatives with respect to the other central coordinate have comparable logarithmic size.",
      "figure_id": "can:wing-figure",
      "pdf_page": 10,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "can:wing-figure",
          "exact_quote": "The two paths are followed outward from a fixed central position.\nMatched indices need not be equally far from the center; their derivatives\nwith respect to the other central coordinate have comparable logarithmic\nsize.",
          "source_object": "fig:0001"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1cm,y=1cm,>=Stealth]\n  \\draw[->] (0,0)--(-5,0);\n  \\draw[->] (0,0)--(5,0);\n  \\foreach \\x in {-4.6,-2,0,2.6,4.6}\n    \\fill (\\x,0) circle (1.5pt);\n  \\node[above] at (0,0) {$q_e$};\n  \\node[above] at (-2,0) {$q_{e-j_m^-}$};\n  \\node[above] at (2.6,0) {$q_{e+j_m^+}$};\n  \\node[above] at (-4.6,0) {left endpoint};\n  \\node[above] at (4.6,0) {right endpoint};\n  \\draw[densely dashed] (-2,-.15)--(-2,-.65)--(2.6,-.65)--(2.6,-.15);\n  \\node[below,align=center] at (0,-.65)\n    {a matched pair: $\\abs{\\log_M L_{j_m^-}^- -\\log_M L_{j_m^+}^+}\\le3/5$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A horizontal axis/line depicting outward paths from a central position labeled $q_e$, with left- and right-pointing arrows along the line. Marked points from left to right: \"left endpoint\", $q_{e-j_m^-}$, $q_e$, $q_{e+j_m^+}$, and \"right endpoint\". Below the points $q_{e-j_m^-}$ and $q_{e+j_m^+}$, an indicated pair is labeled \"a matched pair: $\\left|\\log_M L_{j_m^-}^- - \\log_M L_{j_m^+}^+\\right| \\le 3/5$\"."
    },
    {
      "caption": "Coordinate schematic for the bridge change of variables, not an orbit plot. A forward level is labelled at $y = y_{c}$ by $s$; the eligible slice $D_{s}$ is swept across the square along transverse backward levels to the coordinate $(u,Θ_{s}(u,y))$.",
      "figure_id": "bridge:sweep-figure",
      "pdf_page": 24,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "bridge:sweep-figure",
          "exact_quote": "Coordinate schematic for the bridge change of variables, not\nan orbit plot. A forward level is labelled at $y=y_c$ by $s$; the\neligible slice $D_s$ is swept across the square along transverse\nbackward levels to the coordinate $(u,\\Theta_s(u",
          "source_object": "fig:0002"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=0.82cm,y=0.82cm,>=Latex,font=\\small]\n  \\definecolor{leafblue}{RGB}{28,75,118}\n  \\definecolor{sweeporange}{RGB}{167,82,36}\n  \\draw[gray!65] (0,0) rectangle (7,4.5);\n  \\draw[gray!65,->] (0,0) -- (7.35,0) node[right] {$x$};\n  \\draw[gray!65,->] (0,0) -- (0,4.85) node[above] {$y$};\n  \\draw[gray!55,densely dotted] (0,2.25) -- (7,2.25);\n  \\node[left] at (0,2.25) {$y_c$};\n  \\draw[gray!60,densely dashed] (5.4,0) -- (5.4,4.5);\n  \\node[below] at (5.4,0) {$u$};\n  \\draw[sweeporange,thick]\n    (0.2,1.17) .. controls (2.5,1.26) and (4.6,1.43) .. (6.8,1.51);\n  \\draw[sweeporange,thick]\n    (0.2,2.02) .. controls (2.5,2.13) and (4.6,2.40) .. (6.8,2.46);\n  \\draw[sweeporange,thick]\n    (0.2,2.91) .. controls (2.5,3.02) and (4.6,3.16) .. (6.8,3.24);\n  \\draw[leafblue,very thick]\n    (3.0,0.18) .. controls (2.86,1.35) and (3.18,2.69) .. (3.06,4.33);\n  \\fill[leafblue] (3.07,2.25) circle (1.7pt);\n  \\node[below right,leafblue] at (3.07,2.25) {$s$};\n  \\draw[leafblue,thick,{Bar[width=4pt]}-{Bar[width=4pt]}]\n    (2.95,1.34) -- (3.13,3.11);\n  \\node[left,leafblue,fill=white,inner sep=1pt] at (2.75,3.13) {$D_s$};\n  \\fill[sweeporange] (5.4,2.40) circle (1.7pt);\n  \\node[right,sweeporange,fill=white,inner sep=1pt]\n    at (5.4,2.40) {$\\Theta_s(u,y)$};\n  \\node[above,leafblue] at (3.18,4.32) {forward $Q$-level};\n  \\node[above,sweeporange] at (5.74,3.25) {backward $F$-levels};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "A coordinate diagram in a square region with axes $x$ (horizontal) and $y$ (vertical). A horizontal level is marked at $y_c$ on the vertical axis. A vertical line intersects this level at coordinate $s$, labeled \"forward $Q$-level\", with a vertical segment marked $D_s$. Multiple curves transverse to the vertical line are labeled \"backward $F$-levels\". A point on one of these backward levels is marked and labeled $\\Theta_s(u, y)$, corresponding to horizontal coordinate $u$."
    },
    {
      "caption": "The possible temporal regions in 20; the one-ray picture may also be reflected. A slow region need not be affine. In the bottom picture, excessive loss between the exterior rays would produce the symmetric forbidden triple $(x_{s},m,y_{s})$ used in 21. The diagram describes time regions, not an embedding of the semimetric.",
      "figure_id": "shape:figure",
      "pdf_page": 38,
      "source_evidence": [
        {
          "anchor": "shape:figure",
          "exact_quote": "The possible temporal regions in \\cref{shape:classification};\nthe one-ray picture may also be reflected.  A slow region need not be\naffine.  In the bottom picture, excessive loss between the exterior rays\nwould produce the symmetric forbidd",
          "source_object": "fig:0003"
        }
      ],
      "source_kind": "tikz_pgf",
      "tikz_pgf_source": "\\begin{tikzpicture}[x=1.05cm,y=1cm,>=Latex,font=\\small]\n  \\node[anchor=east] at (-4.4,2.7) {No unit ray};\n  \\draw[gray!65,line width=3pt] (-4,2.7)--(4,2.7);\n  \\node[fill=white,inner sep=2pt] at (0,2.7) {all secants have speed $\\le c_b$};\n  \\node[anchor=east] at (-4.4,1.5) {One unit ray};\n  \\draw[blue!65!black,line width=1.1pt,->] (-1,1.5)--(-4,1.5);\n  \\draw[gray!65,line width=3pt] (-1,1.5)--(4,1.5);\n  \\fill (-1,1.5) circle (1.6pt);\n  \\node[above] at (-2.6,1.5) {unit speed};\n  \\node[above] at (1.5,1.5) {slow half-line};\n  \\node[below] at (-1,1.5) {$a$};\n  \\node[anchor=east] at (-4.4,0) {Two unit rays};\n  \\draw[blue!65!black,line width=1.1pt,->] (-1,0)--(-4,0);\n  \\draw[gray!65,line width=3pt] (-1,0)--(1,0);\n  \\draw[blue!65!black,line width=1.1pt,->] (1,0)--(4,0);\n  \\draw[<->] (-1,.55)--(1,.55) node[midway,above] {$L=b-a>0$};\n  \\foreach \\x in {-3.3,-1,0,1,3.3}{\\fill (\\x,0) circle (1.6pt);}\n  \\node[below] at (-3.3,0) {$x_s=a-s$};\n  \\node[below] at (-1,0) {$a$};\n  \\node[below] at (0,0) {$m$};\n  \\node[below] at (1,0) {$b$};\n  \\node[below] at (3.3,0) {$y_s=b+s$};\n\\end{tikzpicture}",
      "witness_description": "Three horizontal schematic diagrams arranged vertically:\n1. Top (\"No unit ray\"): A single horizontal bar with text \"all secants have speed $\\le c_b$\".\n2. Middle (\"One unit ray\"): A horizontal bar with a boundary point $a$. To the left of $a$, an arrow pointing left labeled \"unit speed\". To the right of $a$, a shaded line labeled \"slow half-line\".\n3. Bottom (\"Two unit rays\"): A horizontal segment with marked points $x_s = a - s$, $a$, $m$, $b$, and $y_s = b + s$. Left-pointing arrow starting at $a$ through $x_s$, right-pointing arrow starting at $b$ through $y_s$. Between $a$ and $b$, a double-headed arrow indicates distance $L = b - a > 0$. Point $m$ lies between $a$ and $b$."
    }
  ],
